Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the thermodynamics structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 826. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 578 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_826(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 826. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^578(S, F) is trivial. QED.
